# top-theorems.txt: one line for each theorem that the audit looks up by name. A name stands on
# one line only. Lines that begin with "#" are comments.
# The audit is scripts/Audit.lean.in, a Lean program that scripts/check.py runs over the compiled
# library. It fails if a name on this list does not exist, is no theorem, or has a statement that
# mentions no declaration of this library.
# Words used below: "choice" is the axiom Classical.choice; the "reach" of a statement is the set
# of declarations of this library on which it rests.
#
# The three forms of a line:
#   closed: the full name; "-", or a number that the reach of what the theorem promises must not
#   exceed; the closed constant or constants below which there must be no choice:
#       NAME  -  CONSTANT
#   general: the full name; the word general. Statement, axioms, reach and syntax are reported,
#   without a verdict on binders, scheme or choice, and without a test that the hypotheses inside
#   the statement can be met together: a pass says that the theorem stands and rests on the three
#   axioms, and nothing on whether any object meets its hypotheses:
#       NAME  general
#   A statement may be a constant whose definition holds further premises. They do not show in the
#   theorem's type; the audit counts them among the "binders after unfolding", and the listing
#   prints them.
#   headline with named binders: the full name; the word headline; the binders in order, separated
#   by " | ", each a constant by its full name applied to earlier binders by position (#0 is the
#   first); "=>"; the predicate at the head of the conclusion; then, each after " | ", all its
#   explicit arguments in order: the word walk (no choice below it: a verdict) or report (where
#   choice is reached: no verdict), a constant of this library, the binders that it is applied to;
#   the word met; one closed witness for each binder, separated by " | ": a numeral for Nat, a
#   definition of this library for one of its types, a theorem of this library for a hypothesis.
#   The audit compares the line with the compiled type as terms.
#
# Here: 0 headline lines, 0 closed lines, 10776 general lines. A name that a document cites may
# still be missing here: it is then compiled, replayed and walked for axioms like every
# declaration, and no more. No limit is set on the reach. A name with characters other than ASCII
# letters, digits, _ ' ! ? and dots cannot stand here.
